# Provenance

Every file below was copied from `PrompTugal/PrompTugal__News__Website` (branch dev, copied 2026-11-26) with its original path. Nothing was deleted at the source. Blanked in the copies: the `append_token` and `sealed` fields of the three inbox cards, and one agent mailbox address (shown as `[contact blanked]`).

Not copied (site code or other agents' files that only mention investors): newsroom/build/features/investors-site.mjs, data/topics/investor/, design/decisions/investor-site-2026-11-07.jpg, desks/synthetic/run-1/runs/investor.json, desks/writer-angelo/img/passes/*investor.png. The guidance rows are in guidance-rows.md.
